GCW — Card

The Dangerous Games Anthology 1, 15 March 2008

15 March 2008
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
The Dangerous Games Anthology 1 1st Mariner Arena — Baltimore, Maryland ---- Dangerous Games 2001 Andrews- The following contest is a Battle Royal for the number one contendership to the World Title! The crowd cheers in anticipation Andrews- To be eliminated, someone must be thrown over the top rope, and BOTH feet must hit the floor. Chamoflauge hits, and the crowd boos. Chameleon walks to the ring, Katanya at his side. He hops into the ring, and glares out at the crowd. Andrews- Introducing the man who drew number one, from Las Vegas, Nevada, he weighs in at 200 pounds. . . Chameleon! Bryan- This man has gone absolutely insane as of late, fans, and it appears as if Katanya has gone crazy with him! I don’t know why she did what she did earlier tonight, but I’ve got to believe that Mike Hardy has paid the ultimate price as a result! Yale- Yeah, Hardy was supposed to be in this match, but as a result of that buried alive match, he’s been taken to a nearby hospital! The Game hits next, and the crowd boos as Mikado walks to the ring. Andrews- And the man who drew number two, from parts unknown, he weighs in at 235 pounds. . . Mikado! Bryan- And it looks like one-half of the former tag champions will be facing him here in the beginning! Mikado steps through the ropes, and the bell rings. The fans cheer the match as it begins. Bryan- Here we go! Thirty men, one will walk out the number one contender to the world title! We’re starting with two, and every two minutes or so another man will enter the ring! Yale- Chameleon goes straight at Mikado, and Mikado takes him down with a leg sweep! Chameleon lands right on the back of his head, and Mikado retreats to the corner! Looks like he’s playing a more conservative game here! Bryan- Smart idea. Chameleon, on the other hand, is still wanting a fight! I don’t know how he can do it after that war with Hardy! And that masked man attacking! Yale- Chameleon runs in again, and Mikado ducks, backdropping him over the top rope! But Chameleon lands on the apron! Mikado turns, and Chameleon hits him with a shoulder block to the gut through the ropes! Bryan- Chameleon leaps to the top rope, as Mikado recovers! Chameleon takes off with a cross body block! But Mikado catches him and turns it into a powerslam! What a move! Yale- That was impressive! Chameleon is really hurting, and it looks like the clock it ticking down! The crowd chants as the clock runs out. . . Three! TWO! ONE!! A siren sounds, as Mikado looks down the ramp. La Grange plays, and the crowd cheers. Jake Jennings walks to the ring, and hops through the ropes. Bryan- Jake Jennings! Jennings is the third man into this match! Chameleon is still down from the powerslam, and Mikado moves right in on Jennings! Mikado and Jennings begin to exchange blows, as Chameleon tries to regain his senses! Yale- Jennings is taking over on Mikado, and he moves him back into the corner! Jennings begins pounding away with hard rights and lefts, beating the smaller Mikado down! Bryan- Chameleon is back to his feet, and he moves to Jennings! But he just shoves Jennings aside, and begins to stomp away! Jennings watches from aside, but now Jennings shoves Chameleon aside and begins to hammer away at Mikado again! Yale- Chameleon is getting frustrated here, and he pulls Jennings off of Mikado! They stare each other down. . . And now both men are pounding away at Mikado! Mikado is being double-teamed here! Bryan- Wait, now Chameleon breaks off, and headbutts Jennings between the eyes! Jennings staggers back, and Chameleon goes to work on him! Mikado is taking a breather in the corner, but is slowly creeping up the turnbuckle! Yale- Chameleon is hammering away at Jennings, but Mikado is up to the top rope! Mikado takes off, and hits a double dropkick on both Jennings and Chameleon! All three men go down! The crowd begins to chant Three! Two! ONE! A buzzer sounds, and the crowd stands to see who is next. Freak on a Leash hits, and the crowd erupts in boos. Shane Lane struts to the ring, flexing on his way. Bryan- Shane Lane! Shane Lane has drawn number four! He failed to capture the US title earlier, but he could win this match now! But he’s got a huge mountain to climb! Yale- This isn’t good, JB! Shane’s got to go through the entire GCW roster to win this thing now! Bryan- Lane steps into the ring, as the three men are all still down! It looks like Jake Jennings is the first one up, and Lane heads for him! Lane fires off a right hand, and another! Jennings goes back down! The crowd boos as Lane poses, looking as if he had taken the three men out himself. Yale- Lane’s doing some posing! All right! Hey, wait, Mikado is back up behind Lane! Lane turns, and Mikado fires off a few quick right hands! Mikado has Lane reeling, and knocks him down with a sharp knife-edge chop! Bryan- Mikado is going to work on Lane! Now Chameleon is back up, and is coming up behind Mikado! But Mikado hears him coming, and he whips around with a flying heel kick! He catches Chameleon right in the face, and Chameleon is down again! The crowd oohs, and cheers Mikado. The they see the clock ticking down. Three! Two! ONE! The siren sounds again, and Demons Surround Me hits. The crowd boos as Capo heads to the ring. Bryan- It looks like the former Television champion is next! Yale- Capo was robbed by that damn Zippy, JB! But hey, Capo can win this battle royal, and go on to face the GCW champion! That would be great! Capo steps into the ring, and heads for Jennings! Lane is being stomped by Mikado, as Chameleon recovers! Bryan- Capo hammers away at Jennings, and Mikado breaks off his attack on Lane! Mikado heads to Capo, and Capo fires a right hand at him! Mikado and Capo are going at it, now, as both Chameleon and Lane get to their feet! Yale- Chameleon pulls himself up with the ropes, and gets to his feet! Lane is up as well, and he charges Chameleon! But Chameleon ducks, and backdrops Lane over the top rope! Bryan- Lane lands on the apron! Chameleon turns around, and Lane hooks him up! Lane lifts Chameleon up with a suplex, and drops him outside to the floor! Chameleon is gone! The crowd cheers, as Katanya runs to console him. She helps Chameleon up and they head to the back. Three! Two! ONE! The siren sounds again, and Paranoid hits. Andrew Hawk walks to the ring, to a mild negative reaction. Yale- It’s the Ace Andrew Hawk! Hawk steps into the ring, and goes after Capo! Mikado breaks off his attack on Capo, as Hawk begins to pound away! Capo fires back with a few rights, as Jake Jennings pulls himself back up! Bryan- Mikado heads for Jennings, who leaps up and rakes Mikado’s eyes! Mikado stumbles to the corner, as Shane Lane moves in behind Jennings! But Jennings turns and nails Lane with a big right hand, sending Lane to the mat! Yale- Wow! Lane pulls himself back up, as Capo and Hawk continue to battle it out! The ring is really getting crowded now, JB! The crowd begins to chant again. . . Three! Two! ONE! The siren sounds, and Break Stuff hits. The crowd explodes, and John Reed heads to the ring. Bryan- The newest member of System-X is here at number seven! John Reed charges into the ring, and goes for Mikado! Reed begins to hammer away at Mikado, as Shane Lane recovers and sneaks up behind Reed! Yale- Lane hits Reed with a double-axe handle from behind, and Mikado takes Reed down with a thrust kick! Andrew Hawk has taken over on Capo, and is pounding away in the corner! Meanwhile, Jennings is taking a breather in the opposite corner! Bryan- Reed is down and being double-teamed by Mikado and Lane! They have Reed up, and are trying to dump them over the top rope! But Jennings runs over and takes Mikado down with a boot to the gut! Yale- Reed rakes Lane’s eyes, and he’s free! Lane stumbles across the ring, as Reed pursues him! Now Andrew Hawk has Capo straddling the top turnbuckle, trying to dump him out to the floor! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, and Real American plays. The new TV champ runs to the ring, full of energy. Bryan- Zippy! The new Television Champ is here! Zippy runs into the ring, and goes right for. . . Andrew Hawk! Zippy takes Hawk down with a dropkick! But now he’s going for Capo! Zippy is trying to toss Capo out of the ring! Yale- But Capo gives him a stiff right hand shot, and Zippy goes down! Capo gets to his feet, and begins stomping away at Zippy! Reed has Shane Lane in the middle of the ring, now, and delivers a big scoop slam! Bryan- Jennings is still pounding away at Mikado! Andrew Hawk is back up, and he gets a cheap shot in on Reed from behind! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, and I Disappear hits. The crowd goes crazy, not expecting Hardy. Yale- What?! How is HE here? Hardy does not emerge. Finally, the masked man from the buried alive match walks down the ramp, his identity still secret. Bryan- Wait a minute, it looks like that masked man that helped Hardy is now taking his place in this battle royal! The masked man hops into the ring, and goes after Hawk! Who is this guy? Yale- I don’t know, but he’s assaulting Andrew Hawk! Capo is still hammering away at Zippy on the far side of the ring, while Shane Lane takes a breather in the corner! Lane finally sees Reed standing up, and heads for him! Bryan- Lane and Reed meet, and Reed connects with a quick right! Lane fights back, and we’ve got a brawl! Finally, Lane takes over, and sends Reed back to the canvas with a stiff right hand! Three! Two! ONE! The siren sounds, and Travis Charger heads to the ring. Yale- Travis Charger is number ten! That’ll be a tough spot for this rookie! Bryan- Charger heads for Mikado! Charger begins hammering away at Mikado, as the masked man now pounds away at Capo! Capo fights back, but the masked man is getting the better of him! Yale- Lane is stomping away at John Reed in the corner, as Jake Jennings and Travis Charger double team Mikado! Wait, Mikado fights out of the corner with a few sharp knife-edge chops! He sends Jennings to the mat! Bryan- Mikado leaps, and connects with a spinning heel kick on Charger! And Charger topples over the top rope! The crowd cheers, as the clock runs down. . . Three! Two! ONE! The siren sounds again, and Tantric begins to play. The crowd boos as Chris Jordan heads for the ring. Yale- All right!! The Cruiserweight and now Tag Team Champion is here! This guy is undefeated, JB! He’s got to be my pick to win the whole thing! Chris Jordan is going to be the number one contender to the world title! Bryan- He’s got a huge mountain to climb, JB! Jordan already had a hellacious match with Toad, who was robbed in my opinion, and now he has nineteen other guys still to come in this match! Yale- Well, Shane Lane and John Reed are still going at it in the corner, and Capo is still catching a beating from the masked man! Wait, John Reed is slowly fighting back on Lane in the corner! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, and Freak on a Leash plays again. Hiroshima Genryu emerges and runs to the ring. Bryan- What? This is an unfair advantage for Lane that his bodyguard is allowed in this match! But Lane may be gone here! Reed takes Lane by the hair, gets a head of steam. . . And tosses Lane over the top rope! Yale- Wait! Lane flies to the floor, but Genryu is right there! Genryu catches Lane, and rolls him back into the ring! That’s why Genryu is here! The crowd boos as Genryu steps through the ropes. But as he gets in, Reed charges and clotheslines him right back to the floor! Bryan- Whoa! John Reed just took Hiroshima Genryu right out! But he served his purpose, and helped Lane stay in this match! It may be a bit unfair, but there’s nothing we can do about it! Yale- Chris Jordan is going after the masked man, now, and has him down in the corner! And now he’s going for the mask! Chris Jordan is trying to unmask this man! But the masked man boots Jordan between the legs, and Jordan is down! Bryan- I may not like Chris Jordan that much, but quite frankly, I want to know who this guy is! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds again, and Damien Edge walks to the ring. Yale- Damien Edge is in at unlucky number thirteen! We’ve got ten men in the ring now! It’s utter chaos! Zippy is hammering away at Capo again, while Reed and Mikado have it out! Shane Lane is taking it easy on the far side again! Bryan- Edge hops into the ring, and he’s greeted by Jake Jennings! Jennings pounds away at Damien Edge, steps back, and clotheslines him over the top! But Damien holds on, and lands on the apron! Yale- Mikado is ripping away at the masked man now, trying to reveal his face! But the masked man is holding on to the mask for dear life! Who the hell is that, JB? Bryan- Now Damien Edge is in control of Jennings! But Jennings shoves Edge away, and kicks him right in the face! Edge tips over the top rope and falls to the floor below! The crowd cheers as they begin to chant. . . Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, and Last Resort hits. The crowd cheers as Stone walks to the ring. Yale- Stone! What were you saying about unfair advantages, JB? Stone is here at number fourteen, and now he’s got Zippy to side with in there! He’s in the ring, and he goes for Capo, who’s working over Zippy! Bryan- Ten men in the ring, and it’s difficult to see what’s going on! Stone and Zippy are double teaming Capo, and trying to toss him out! But Andrew Hawk makes the save, taking Stone out with a chop block! Yale- John Reed and Chris Jordan are going at it, now, and Reed is getting the better of the exchange! Meanwhile, that masked man is assaulting Mikado! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, and Pleasant Dreams hits. Dream heads to the ring with slight boos. Bryan- It’s Dream! Number fifteen is Dream! We’re halfway through this battle royal! Dream hops into the ring, and is met by Shane Lane! Lane hammers away, and takes Dream right down with a kick to the back of the knee! Yale- Chris Jordan has stopped Reed with a snap suplex, and Andrew Hawk is battling it out with Stone! Zippy attacks Hawk from behind now, saving Stone! That’s hardly fair! Bryan- Jake Jennings is now squaring off with Capo, and beating him down in the corner! This is something else, Dave! Wait, Zippy is going up top! Zippy is on the turnbuckle, and he takes off! Yale- Zippy connects with a suicide dive, taking out Jennings and Capo at the same time! All three men are down! But that’s dangerous to leave your feet in a battle royal! But then, this is Dangerous Games! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, and More Pain hits. LeStatt Knight emerges, heading to the ring. Bryan- Newcomer LeStatt Knight is in at number sixteen! Knight hops in, and heads to Dream! Shane Lane has disappeared again in the crowd here, and that’s a smart move! Yale- Shane’s keeping a low profile! But Jake Jennings has spotted him, and is pounding away at Lane in the corner! Jennings wants to win this thing, JB! And he could do it! Bryan- Knight is in control of Dream, and is choking him out in the middle of the ring! Meanwhile, Mikado and John Reed appear to be going at it, while Chris Jordan attempts to unmask the masked man once again! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, and Welcome to the Jungle plays. The crowd leaps to their feet, cheering for Trash. Bryan- Trash is number seventeen! And listen to this reception! Trash turned on Shadowedge and the rest of the CWU earlier tonight, and now he’s here to become the number one contender! Yale- Trash hops into the ring, and sets his sights on Chris Jordan! Trash begins pounding away at Jordan, while the masked man makes a subtle escape from Jordan’s mask-tearing rampage! Bryan- But Andrew Hawk sees this masked man, and begins to stomp away! The masked man is in trouble, as is Shane Lane! Jake Jennings is trying to toss Shane Lane out! But Lane has the turnbuckle hooked, and he’s not going anywhere! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, and Bawitiba hits. The crowd cheers as Dan Rivero heads to the ring. Yale- One half of the new tag team champions, Dan Rivero, is in at number eighteen! Rivero climbs in, and The Man heads for Mikado! Rivero hits Mikado from behind, and this thing isn’t over! Bryan- Rivero and Mikado going at it for the second time tonight, while Capo assaults Stone in the corner! Jordan is going at it with John Reed again, as Andrew Hawk takes Jake Jennings down, saving Shane Lane from elimination! Yale- Oh, come on, Jennings wasn’t going to eliminate Lane anyway! Mikado has taken control of Rivero, which is impressive from the man that entered at number two! Bryan- Zippy is going at it with Capo, still, and we have fourteen men in the ring right now! Chris Jordan helps Capo out, and they’re double-teaming Zippy! Capo tries to toss Zippy out, but Zippy doesn’t go over the top! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, and the Game hits. Assassin One walks to the ring, as the crowd boos. Bryan- You want to talk about unfair, Dave, now both of the former tag champions are in the ring! And they’re both pissed at Dan Rivero! Yale- Assassin One heads for Rivero, and he and Mikado begin double teaming one of the men who took their titles tonight! Assassin One and Mikado are pounding away at Rivero, while trying to toss him out! Bryan- But Jake Jennings makes the save, assaulting Assassin One from behind! Meanwhile, Dream is assaulting Shane Lane! Dream charges in and hits Lane with a cross body! But Lane catches him, falls into the ropes, and drops Dream over top! Dream is eliminated! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, and Fuel hits. The crowd leaps to their feet, cheering loudly for the US champ. Yale- Luke Hanson! The guy who shouldn’t be US champ is here! And he shouldn’t even get to be in this match after the cheap win he got earlier! Bryan- That’s arguable! But Lane is catching a beating from Trash right now! Hanson ignores Lane, and goes after the masked man! John Reed and Hanson are keeping close already, as Reed and Hanson begin double-teaming the masked man! Yale- But Andrew Hawk attacks Reed from behind! And Shane Lane is coming back on Trash, throwing some hard right hands! But Trash is hanging in there with the man who should be US champion! Bryan- Oh, come on! Hawk is beating on Reed, but Hanson runs over to help him out! Hanson begins pounding on Hawk, but the masked man hits him with a low blow from behind! Who the hell is that guy?! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds again, and Dimensioner runs to the ring. Yale- Another member of the old CWU! Dimensioner is here! Dimensioner heads into the ring, and goes right for Trash! Dimensioner and Shane Lane begin to double-team Trash in the corner, but Hanson and Reed head over to help him out! Bryan- Hanson and Reed pick off Lane and Dimensioner, but Dimensioner fights back on Reed! Hanson and Lane are going at it again! Lane is catching a beating from Hanson, as they fight into the corner! Yale- Zippy and Capo are going at it again, as Mikado and Assassin One continue their two on one assault on Rivero! They’re trying to toss him out over the top, but Rivero is barely holding on! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, and Bawitiba plays again. The crowd cheers as the other half of the tag champs heads for the ring. Bryan- Chris Van Burren! The other half of the Real Deal is here, and he heads right for Assassin One and Mikado! They’ve almost got Rivero out, but Van Burren runs in with a high cross body to their backs! Yale- Whoa! All four men topple over the top rope! Assassin One, Mikado, and Van Burren all hit the floor! But Rivero manages to land on the apron, and he's still in this match! The crowd cheers, as the frustrated Assassin One and Mikado begin attacking Van Burren on the outside. Bryan- Van Burren saved Rivero, but ended up sacrificing himself in the process! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, and Fat Lip hits. The crowd explodes in cheers again as Toxic heads for the ring. Bryan- Toxic! Toxic is here at number twenty three! Toxic hops into the ring, and goes for Shane Lane, who has gained control over Hanson! Yale- You’ve got to hand it to Lane! He and Jake Jennings have been in there for forty eight minutes! Jennings is down and catching a breather on the far side of the ring, while Toxic and Hanson now work over Lane! Bryan- You do have to be impressed by the stamina of Shane Lane and Jake Jennings! They are really impressing me here! Andrew Hawk looks like he’s taking a beating from Capo, while Zippy and Stone watch from a corner! Yale- Stone looks like he’s explaining all this to Zippy! And Zippy looks like he’s confused as to why everyone’s in the ring! I can’t believe this guy is our Television champion now! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, and Chamoflauge hits. The crowd boos in confusion, as Chameleon walks out again. Bryan- What? He’s already been eliminated! Yale- Wait a minute! I have word that he’s been entered as both Chameleon. . . and now Demented! This is Demented in the ring now! And he’s going for that masked man! The masked man sees Demented, and escapes under the bottom rope! Bryan- Demented follows the masked man out under the ropes, and chases him around the ring! The masked man slips back under the ropes, but is followed by Demented! But the masked man is stopped by a boot from Chris Jordan! Yale- Demented catches up to the masked man, and grabs that mask! Demented pulls the mask off, and the astonished face below causes the crowd to cheer in amazement. . . Bryan- BRANSON KING?!?!? Branson King was the masked man! I don’t believe it! I thought he had left GCW forever! But he’s here in this battle royal! Yale- Demented can’t believe it, either! Branson King gets to his feet, and begins going at it with Demented! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, and Out With the Old hits. The crowd goes crazy, adding to the pandemonium as Toad heads for the ring. Bryan- The former Hard*core champion is in at number twenty five! Toad slides into the ring, and now all three members of System-X are in the ring at once! Yale- That is unfair right there, JB! Hanson and Lane are still going at it, while Reed and Trash double team Dimensioner! Toad climbs in, and goes right for Jordan! Bryan- Toad hammers away at Jordan, and pounds him right to the mat! Toad is on fire! Jake Jennings comes up behind him, however, and stops him with a boot to the kidneys! Yale- Jordan crawls away from Toad, leaving Jennings to do the work! There are seventeen men in the ring now, JB! And here comes another! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, and Liberi Fatale hits. The crowd boos as Shadowedge heads to the ring. Bryan- Another member of the old CWU is here! Shadowedge climbs in, and goes straight for Trash! Trash cost Shadowedge his match earlier, and now he’s looking for revenge! Yale- Capo has been in this match for forty six minutes now, and is still going strong! He’s going at it with Stone now, but Zippy is pounding on him from behind! Capo is in real trouble here! Bryan- Hey, wait a minute! Someone has run out from the back! That’s. . .Big Skails, new GCW signee! Skails heads for the ring, and hops onto the apron! He reaches in, and grabs Stone! He pulls Stone over the top, and Stone falls to the floor! Yale- Whoa! Stone is gone! And Capo is staring at Skails in disbelief! Last I checked, Skails and Capo are brothers, JB! But Capo isn’t paying attention, and Zippy dropkicks him from behind! Capo flies over the top rope, and is gone! The crowd cheers, as Capo stands up. He shoves Skails away, and heads to the back. Skails follows, pleading with Capo. Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, as the Beautiful People hits. The crowd boos as Katanya heads to the ring. Bryan- Katanya’s in this match, too?! She heads into the ring, and goes for Branson King! King is fighting off both Katanya and Demented now, but seems to be fighting a losing battle! Yale- There are only three men left to be introduced, JB! Katanya is pounding on King, while Demented holds him back! John Reed, Hanson, and Toad have regrouped, and are standing in the corner! Bryan- Andrew Hawk has been in there since number six! He’s going at it with LeStatt Knight, and they are battling it out hard in the corner! Hawk takes Knight by the head, and tosses him right over the top rope! Yale- Whoa! Knight is gone, and Hawk is gloating about it! But Knight is back up on the apron! Knight reaches in, and pulls Hawk out over the top as well! Both men are gone! And they’re fighting on the outside! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, as Hawk and Knight battle to the back. Original Prankster hits, and Justin Sane heads for the ring. Bryan- Justin Sane is number twenty eight! Good draw here for Sane! He heads right for Zippy, and begins hammering away! System-X is still huddled in the corner, waiting for the right moment! Yale- I don’t like this, JB! Wait, System-X explodes out of the corner, taking people down left and right! Reed floors Demented and Branson King! Toad takes down Shadowedge! Hanson goes after Lane again! Bryan- Wait, Toad has Dimensioner lined up, and he clotheslines him right over the top to the floor! Toad eliminates Dimensioner! Those two have had a long-running feud, and Toad just got the better of Dimensioner there! Three! Two! ONE! The buzzer sounds, and Who Let the Dogs Out hits. Big Dawg makes his way to the ring. Bryan- Big Dawg! Number twenty nine is Big Dawg! This returning athlete had some poignant words for the President a while back, but still managed to get a good draw! Yale- Big Dawg steps into the ring, and there’s only one man left to be introduced! Big Dawg heads for Jake Jennings, and they begin to slug it out! Trash is going at it with Shadowedge again, as Branson King and Dan Rivero battle it out! Bryan- Wait a minute! Who’s that?! Dan ‘Da Man’ Taylor is here! Taylor runs into the ring, and shoves Branson King out of the way! Taylor rears back, and clotheslines Rivero out to the floor! Does that count?! Yale- The referees are sending Rivero to the back! But Taylor runs out to catch him, and we’ve got a brawl! Taylor has assaulted Rivero! What the hell is that about? Three! Two!! ONE!!! The buzzer sounds for the final time, and the Establishment theme hits. The crowd boos like crazy as President Caldera emerges and slowly heads to the ring. Bryan- Oh, I should have seen this one coming! We knew President Caldera was in this match, and it only makes sense that he entered himself at number thirty! But everyone has been introduced now, and one of these sixteen men will go on to face the GCW champion! Yale- This is it, JB! One of these sixteen will win it all! Think of what’s at stake here! Caldera is taking his time getting into the ring, and is just watching from below! Bryan- Trash is going at it with Shadowedge again, as Chris Jordan and Toad battle it out! John Reed is taking on Justin Sane, while Jake Jennings and Demented hammer away at each other! Yale- Shane Lane and Branson King are still focused on each other, as King keeps Lane down and in trouble in the corner! Hold on a minute, Toad has bailed out of the ring through the ropes, and is sneaking up on Caldera! Bryan- Toad takes Caldera from behind, and rolls him into the ring! The crowd goes crazy. Bryan- Toad rolls in behind him, but John Reed and Luke Hanson have spotted the President! System-X has the President cornered! And they’re going to town on him! The President is in real trouble here! Yale- But Demented takes out Hanson from behind! And Shadowedge takes out Reed, leaving Toad and Caldera! Caldera tries to fight back, but Toad is too much! Bryan- Wait a minute, Katanya is raking at Zippy! Katanya is really ripping away at Zippy. . . But Zippy lifts Katanya up, and simply drops her over the top rope! Katanya is gone! Yale- Toxic is fighting with Jennings now, with Toxic taking the advantage! Jake Jennings has been in there longer than anyone, folks! And Shane Lane entered right after him! Bryan- Chris Jordan spots Toxic, and hits him from behind with a flying dropkick! Toxic goes down, and Jordan goes to work! Big Dawg and Justin Sane are battling it out, while Toad continues to punish the President! Yale- Branson King is taking a breather on the outside, but he has not been eliminated! Wait, Trash has picked off Big Dawg, and has him halfway over the top rope! Here comes King back into the ring to help! Bryan- And Chris Jordan! All three men finally manage to dump Big Dawg over the top, and he’s gone! Fourteen men left in the ring! Caldera is down in the corner, but Demented has come to his aid, and has a sleeper hold locked on Toad from behind! Yale- Hold on, Justin Sane is hammering away at Hanson! But Hanson gives Sane a shove, and connects with a big boot to the face! Sane goes for a ride over the top rope and out to the floor! Justin Sane is gone! Bryan- Jake Jennings is fighting with Toxic on the far side now, as Branson king and Trash go at it! This has been spectacular, Dave! John Reed and Shadowedge are going at it, as Shane Lane fights with Chris Jordan! Yale- Toxic and Zippy are going at it, but here comes Demented! Demented hits a dropkick to Toxic’s back, and Toxic falls into Zippy! Both men fall over the top, but Zippy lands on the apron! Bryan- But Toxic hits the ground! Toxic has been eliminated! The crowd boos in disappointment. Bryan- Toad is slowly recovering from that sleeper, but President Caldera is up and taking advantage of it! Caldera hammers away at the helpless Toad, as Luke Hanson and Shane Lane have singled each other out once again! Yale- Wait a minute, Trash has Zippy straddling the top rope! Zippy is almost out. . . But he rakes Trash’s eyes, and Trash falls back! Zippy stays on the top turnbuckle, and takes off with a ZIP FLIP! Bryan- But Trash catches him in midair! Caldera’s smile having faded into a look of total sincerity, tucks the microphone under his arm and begins applauding upwards towards the crowd. That I Quit match was one of the most brutal matches I’ve ever seen, and now they’re getting ready for round two! Bryan: They’ve lasted through 28 other wrestlers to get to this point! They’re on the brink of a victory they both desperately need! Murray and Knight, who’s it gonna be?! Neither man speaks a word as they gasp for air, trying to summon whatever strength they have left. Suddenly they both break forward, trading epic blows in the center of the ring. The crowd explodes, snapping photos furiously as the exchange rages. Bryan: And here we go! They’re trading huge right hands, but the adrenaline is flowing so high I don’t think either man is feeling pain right now! Yale: They know how huge this moment is, JB! Their entire careers are on the line, this is the opportunity of a lifetime and only one of them can win it! Murray begins to take over on Knight, then backs into the ropes. He bounces back in, but Knight ducks and connects with a knee to the midsection. Doubled over, Murray finds himself trapped between Knight’s legs as he’s lifted into the air! Knight pulls him into the powerbomb and rushes towards the ropes, flinging Murray down and out of the ring with all his might. Bryan: POWERBOMB! Murray’s—no! Murray locks his legs around Knight’s skull and pulls him over the ropes. The crowd explodes as Murray curls onto the apron, delivering a hurricanrana and sending Knight toppling towards the ground! Knight hits the apron and bounces off, but once again hooks the bottom rope and clings perilously above the floor. Yale: He’s alive! Murray countered, but Knight hangs on! Hang on, LeStatt! Bryan: This place is going wild! Knight’s almost gone! Frantically getting back to his feet, Murray begins stomping mercilessly at Knight’s ribcage, desperately trying to force Knight to the ground. But Knight’s hands cling to the rope, refusing to relent despite the incredible punishment being dealt. Knight suddenly swings up with his free leg, kicking Murray’s legs out from under him! The crowd gasps as Murray hits the apron, but he too holds on, and both men roll back into the ring. Bryan: Oh God! Man, Murray HAD Knight right where he wanted him! And then for a moment it looked like Knight might send Murray out, but both men are back in safely! Yale: Jeez! My heart is racing, JB, I can barely breathe! Bryan: London is absolutely rocking right now as these two men continue to beat the hell out of one another, but neither man can seem to get the edge! They both scramble back to their feet, but Knight gets there first and stuns Murray with a right. He shoves Murray back into the ropes and catches him on the rebound with a pickup from the side. But Murray flips over, landing on his feet and then twirling Knight into position for a tombstone. Knight kicks his legs and shifts his weight up, causing Murray to topple over backwards and allowing Knight to land on his feet! Knight adjusts Murray in front of him and drops, driving Murray’s head into the mat. Bryan: TOMBSTONE! Knight countered and piledrove Murray intro the canvas! Yale: YES! This is it! This match just ended, JB! Bryan: I think Murray is out, Dave! You might be right! The crowd boos mightily as Knight sits on the mat, frantically sucking in air. Knocked senseless, Murray tries to move but can’t tell which way is up. Knight slowly picks himself back up and towers over Murray, savoring his helplessness. Knight turns and begins to untie the top turnbuckle pad, tossing it ominously beside Murray’s head. Bryan: Oh, no, what’s Knight doing now? Haven’t you done enough?! Yale: He’s going to enjoy himself before sending Murray away, JB! He’s going to savor this moment, because he’s about to win Dangerous Games! Bryan: He’s playing with fire here! He needs to act while Murray is out, this could come back to bite him… Knight helps Murray back to his feet and watches with sinister amusement as Murray struggles to stand. Knight grabs him by the head and drives his face viciously into the exposed turnbuckle, eliciting a cry of pain from Murray. Bryan: Oh, God, come on! Someone needs to put an end to this! Yale: It’s anything goes, remember? Bryan: But Murray is helpless here, and Knight could do some very real damage! Yale: He’s just picking up where he left off at WorldWide 100! Murray slumps to a knee, but Knight goes right back to it, driving his face into the steel once again. A massive cut forms on Murray’s forehead, hemorrhaging blood as his body begins to quake in pain. Knight sees the blood and only intensifies his attack, slamming Murray’s face into the turnbuckle twice more before Murray slumps back to the mat. Bryan: Good God, this is heinous. Knight is simply assaulting Andy Murray here, and now Murray is busted open! He’s bleeding and he’s bleeding badly, and Knight’s still not done yet! Yale: We’re watching the complete and total dissection of Andy Murray, JB! Where are Murray’s righteous morals, where is his sportsmanship when he needs it? It won’t help him in this situation! LeStatt Knight plays by no rules, and as long as that’s the case Andy Murray won’t truly be able to top him! Lifting Murray out of a pool of his own blood, Knight whips him towards the opposite corner. But Murray can’t stay on his feet, slumping to the mat before he reaches the turnbuckle. Knight stomps over to him as he tries to crawl away, and Knight lifts him into the corner. Knight holds him up with a hand around his neck, staring at his own reflection in the shining blood on Murray’s face. Bryan: I think I’m going to be sick, this is the true, disgusting LeStatt Knight on display here. Yale: Admit it, you’ve missed him. Bryan: No, I really, truly have not. Yale: Well, like it or not, Andy Murray seems to have awoken something deep within LeStatt Knight and his true colors are showing here! Knight reaches back and delivers a slap to the face of Murray, who doesn’t respond but is suddenly able to stand on his own power. Knight inexplicably climbs through the ropes and drops out to the floor, burrowing under the ring apron. Bryan: Oh, what’s he doing now? I shudder to think what he’s after… Oh, God… Knight emerges brandishing a brand new sledgehammer. The crowd cries out as Knight slides back into the ring, stroking the hammer as he stares at a wobbly Murray. Yale: Like I said, finishing what he started at WorldWide! Bryan: Someone get that thing away from him! He’ll cave in Murray’s skull like he almost did at WorldWide! Knight readies the hammer for a swing, but the swing never comes. Instead Knight sets the hammer harmlessly at Murray’s feet, the kneels a few feet away from him, pointing to his jaw. Bryan: Oh, what is this?! Yale: He’s giving Murray another chance to use the hammer! Murray refused to do it last week, I don’t think he’ll do it this time! Bryan: I promise you he won’t! Knight is just taunting him! Blood dripping off the tip of his nose, Murray stoops down and picks up the hammer. Knight pleads with him to strike, but Murray tosses the hammer out of the ring without another thought. Knight laughs and stands, shaking his head in disappointment. Yale: That was stupid! One swing and he could have won this match! Bryan: That’s not how Andy Murray wants it, Dave! He wants to win this thing fair or not at all! Yale: Then he’s not gonna win it at all! Knight steps forward to continue the assault, but Murray suddenly bursts forward. He grabs Knight in a bear hug and hoists him off the mat as he charges across the ring, planting him with Newtonian force against the turnbuckle. The power behind the blow causes the steel post to give, tilting out at 45 degrees! The ropes pull taut as the two men lie in a heap beneath the leaning post, and the crowd cries out in awe. Bryan: OH MY GOD! MURRAY! Murray just exploded! He rammed Knight against the corner and he damn near broke the ring! Yale: Who’s the idiot that put the ring back together?! That was almost very bad! Bryan: That steel post is tilted out, the ring ropes are barely holding things together! That was a ton of weight and strength colliding with the turnbuckle, and the ring couldn’t take it! Yale: And where the hell did that come from out of Murray?! Where did he get the strength?! Bryan: Murray has lost a ton of blood, he’s been bludgeoned into oblivion by LeStatt, but he still damn near broke Knight’s back! This man will not quit, he will not let LeStatt take this moment away from him! Sickened with blood loss but slowly regaining his senses, Murray grabs Knight by the hair and clumsily picks him up. Murray begins with a right hand to the jaw, the feel of which seems to give him new strength. He drives another fist into Knight’s face, steadily growing more powerful. The "MURR-AY! MURR-AY!" chant returns in force as Murray backs Knight into the ropes. Bryan: And look at this! Murray is fighting back! Murray has Knight rocked! Yale: Where is this coming from?! Murray whips Knight to the other side, and Knight bounces back into a massive powerslam from Murray. The crowd explodes and Murray pulls him right back up again, sending him back to the ropes. Knight bounces in again, and this time Murray delivers a kick to the gut. He hooks Knight into a fisherman’s position, then lifts him high into the air. Bryan: HIGHLAND HANGOVER! Yale: NO!!! Murray stalls, bringing the crowd to new heights before driving Knight into the mat. The fans go absolutely crazy as Murray remains on the mat, exhausted, next to a motionless and devastated LeStatt. Bryan: HE GOT HIM! Murray nails the Highland Hangover! Unbelievable! I don’t know where Andy Murray gets his power from, but he’s summoning it from some deep, deep reserve right now! Yale: Get up, LeStatt, get up! Bryan: Could he do it?! Could Andy Murray pull it off?! Knight has brutalized him tonight but he’s got a chance! Suddenly feeling a burst of energy, Murray rolls up to his feet and pumps his fist, sending the already screaming crowd into an absolute frenzy. He lifts Knight up and shoulders him, walking him towards the ropes. But Knight drops down behind him and delivers a chop block, cutting Murray down to his knees. Bryan: Oh, damnit! I thought Murray had Knight there, but somehow Knight is still fighting! Yale: Even the Highland Hangover won’t stop him tonight, JB! Knight wants this more than anything in the world! Both men struggle to regain their composure, but Knight is still paralyzed by the effects of the fisherman buster. Favoring his knee heavily but still moving, Murray lifts himself up and wipes the blood out of his eyes. Bryan: Murray’s back up but I think Knight bought himself a little bit of time! Yale: I sure hope it’s enough! Murray’s moving again! Bryan: I don’t know if Knight can shake that off before Murray takes advantage! Murray shakes the pain out of his knee and pulls Knight back up. He stands Knight in the center of the ring and hits the ropes. He bounces in with a forearm to the face, but Knight doesn’t go down. Dismayed, Murray backs into the ropes again, this time flying in with a jumping lariat. But Knight ducks and catches Murray, unleashing a bestial scream as he lifts Murray into a guerilla press. Bryan: OH MY GOD! Knight has Murray up! He’s got him all the way up! Yale: That’s almost three hundred pounds! Bryan: I don’t believe it, Knight’s got Murray caught in a press! HE’S GOT HIM! Knight charges the edge and throws Murray with all his might. But his arms thrust only air, as Murray drops off behind him and holds on to Knight’s waist for dear life. Bryan: NO! MURRAY ESCAPES! Knight rears around with a back elbow, hitting Murray hard. Murray doesn’t let go, and Knight connects with the other elbow. Murray’s grip loosens and Knight whips around with a massive swinging clothesline, but Murray ducks and wraps Knight up. He delivers a towering belly to belly suplex, so high that Knight is able to flip through and land on his feet. Bryan: KNIGHT COUNTERS! Knight landed on his feet out of that HUGE suplex! Murray is stunned! Murray charges Knight, but this time it’s Knight that traps Murray. He lifts Murray up and delivers a huge belly to belly of his own, tossing Murray hard into the broken turnbuckle. Murray’s careening weight causes the ring post to give, and the ring ropes snap off with a violent reverberation. The post topples down to the ground, with Andy Murray lying on top of it. Bryan: OH MY GOD! THE RING EXPLODED! Yale: YES! YES! YES! YES! YES! Bryan: MURRAY HIT THE GROUND! OH MY GOD! The crowd explodes in absolute shock. The officials crowd around the carnage, jaws agape, exchanging confused and stunned glances. The ring finally stops shaking just in time for the officials to call for the bell, sending a deafening roar up in the stands. ANDY MURRAY ELIMINATED Yale: HE’S DONE IT! KNIGHT HAS DONE IT! Bryan: THERE’S THE BELL! LeSTATT KNIGHT HAS WON DANGEROUS GAMES!!!! "O’Fortuna" cues up ominously as Knight slowly pulls himself to his feet. He slowly surveys the wreckage, finding that half the ring is missing and Andy Murray is nowhere to be seen. Slowly he realizes what has happened and he throws his arms into the air in mighty triumph. Bryan: LeStatt Knight has done it! Damn it all, LeStatt Knight has won Dangerous Games! Yale: I have never in my life seen that before, JB! The power of that move completely broke the ring! Bryan: There was so much power in the ring between these two that the ring couldn’t handle it! And Andy Murray suffered the consequences! Damnit, that’s not fair! I can’t believe what I’m seeing! Yale: Believe it, Knight beat Murray, JB! The camera moves to Murray, who doesn’t move from his spot on the ground. Medics begin to crowd around him as Knight mounts the last complete turnbuckle in the ring, savoring the fury of the British crowd. Bryan: I am stunned! Andy Murray has never lost a big-time match, but Knight somehow beat him here! But I can’t believe how it happened! There’s going to be some controversy here! Yale: Controversy?! I don’t think so! LeStatt Knight launched Murray so hard that nothing was going to stop him! And that’s exactly how Knight approached this match, JB! Knight came in here on fire and NOTHING was going to prevent him from winning! Bryan: That was an epic war between Knight and Murray once again, and this time it’s Knight with the monumental victory! LeStatt Knight has got himself a shot at the GCW World Heavyweight Championship! Damnit all to hell! Knight steps down and walks to the ropeless side of the ring. He stares down at Murray as he is tended to below, his eyes glassy and his chest heaving. Bryan: Oh, come on, leave him be! You’ve done enough, damnit! Yale: Has there ever been a greater moment in GCW history?! LeStatt Knight has gone through 29 other wrestlers to win Dangerous Games one year after finishing second! LeStatt Knight isn’t second any more, JB! He’s first! Bryan: If he truly wants to be first he’s got another match to win at NC-17! LeStatt Knight will be in the main event against the GCW World Champion with the title on the line, and he’ll have a chance for his THIRD World Title reign! But for tonight he can celebrate one of the biggest victories in one of the damnedest Dangerous Games matches I have ever seen! With the fans still in hysterics Knight remains in the ring, listening to the frightening tones of his music. The lights lower in grim recognition of his victory, and Knight drops to a knee, lost in thought. Yale: He’s done it, JB! He’s climbed back to the top of the mountain and he’s claimed his rightful place in the main event of the biggest show of the year. This is the only way things could have ended! Bryan: I’m disgusted. LeStatt Knight did things no human being should do to another, and Andy Murray is suffering the consequences. That man down there wrestled his heart out and gave absolutely everything he had, and if there were any justice in the world he would be getting the title shot he so desperately deserves! Yale: But justice is for suckers, JB, this is the real world and it belongs to LeStatt Knight! Andy Murray just learned life’s most painful lesson, and if he’s smart he’ll take it to heart! Bryan: God, LeStatt Knight has done some unspeakable things to Andy Murray in the past few weeks, and I know Murray will never, ever forget this! I know many people believed this was Murray’s night to earn the title shot that everyone agrees is inevitable, but LeStatt Knight is the one standing tall! Knight places his hands on the middle rope, still trying to collect himself. He looks out through vacant eyes at the camera, holding its gaze until the shot moves out to the crowd. Bryan: Fans, LeStatt Knight now has a date with World Champion, Jordan White at NC-17 in the biggest main event of the year. Yale: God, that’s going to be an unbelievable showdown, JB! And if the LeStatt Knight we saw these last two weeks shows up for NC-17, I don’t know if White will be able to overcome him! Bryan: I still cannot believe what I just saw. Fans, we’re out of time from Dangerous Games 2008! Thank you to everyone who traveled to London to be with us tonight, and thank you to each and every one of you for watching on Pay-Per-View! For our Spanish Announce team, Chris Storm and Markus Stone, for Kevin Jacobs and David Yale, this is James Bryan saying good night… LeStatt Knight has won! The show fades out as LeStatt Knight thrusts his arms into the air once more, and the crowd closes the show with one last cry of anger, fear and despair………
